# GodSim building art report

**23 November 2026, branch `art/buildings`.** The catalog covers all 75 building types, five races and ten ages. The work list supplies 935 building entries and 23 wonder entries. Its age bands expand to 50 individually modelled Town Centers, 100 houses, 875 other buildings and nine wonders. Each race gets two houses per age. The four shared wonders use neutral models with bindings for all five races.

## Coordinator repair round 2

Round 2 starts at `7e905bb`. The audit groups all non-wonder models by race, building type and variant, then tests every possible direction within each group. It includes individual House variants and every Town Center age within its family. Each indexed LOD0 triangle corner counts once per occurrence. All three coordinates are rounded to 0.001 before comparing membership in the sibling’s position set. The test compares integer counts with the 60% and 80% thresholds before display rounding. [Coordinator list](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/rc4/coordinator_families.json) · [Exact checkpoint counts](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/rc4/checkpoint_pairs.json)

The checkpoint had 3,230 ordered comparisons across 285 multi-model families. Of 101 affected families, 27 had a maximum above 80%, including the Human Granary at 3,468/3,492 (99.31%). The other 74 families contained 227 distinct pairs over 60% in at least one direction. An independent critic opened 32 sheets containing 908 unpainted 64-pixel views and identified 154 pairs that needed changes and 73 whose construction could already be distinguished. Each decision is tied to the original GLB hashes. The two rounding boundary cases were the Saurian Shrine at exactly 80% and the Orc Hunting Lodge at 1,408/2,346 (60.017%). [Pair decisions, reasons and evidence](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/rc4/triage.json) · [Checkpoint comparison sheets](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/rc4/triage/)

<!-- RC4 FINAL RESULTS -->

The repair changed **154 LOD0 meshes in 87 families**. All 27 mandatory families pass in every direction. Their highest remaining comparison is 58.960% (1,224/2,076), from `bld_human_bank_future_v0` to `bld_human_bank_classical_v0`. The highest comparison involving any revised GLB is 19.167% (276/1,440). The LOD1 ceiling is exceeded by 0 of 1,034 models. [Final all-pairs audit and changed IDs](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/rc4/final_audit.json)

The catalog-wide peak is **79.887% (1,275/1,596)** for `bld_human_shrine_future_v0` to `bld_human_shrine_ancient_v0` and `bld_human_shrine_future_v0` to `bld_human_shrine_classical_v0`. Exactly 88 directions in 56 distinct pairs from 23 families exceed 60%. Another 17 checkpoint exceptions dropped below the ceiling when one of the models was rebuilt for a different comparison. Both GLBs in every surviving pair match the bytes the independent critic viewed.

| Race | Listed families | Mandatory families rebuilt | Moderate families rebuilt | Changed GLBs |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Human | 30 | 12 | 15 | 42 |
| Elf | 9 | 1 | 8 | 16 |
| Dwarf | 19 | 6 | 7 | 26 |
| Orc | 20 | 3 | 17 | 35 |
| Saurian | 23 | 5 | 13 | 35 |

Independent package critics recorded 60 new package rounds and 171 model evaluations. 16 distinct models received a package hold during this repair. 0 received a fourth or later individual evaluation in round two. The lowest scores among changed models are silhouette 91, identity 90, construction 92, charm 90, colour 91, technical 99. The full score table and historical escalations follow below.

<!-- END RC4 FINAL RESULTS -->

The cross-family review sent nine models in seven listed families back to their builders. The Saurian Future Mine gained an exposed ore bench, chute and tracked hauler. The Saurian Future Granary now feeds a sorting belt from graduated hoppers. Three Saurian Temples use an open stone altar court, processional colonnade and daylight nave to distinguish them from their Town Centers. The Classical Orc Fishing Hut gained hanging net racks and stepped quays, and its Woodcutter Camp now carries a trunk on sawbucks beside a loaded cart. The Classical Human Barracks gained a training court and supported dormitory gallery, and the Classical Blacksmith gained an open masonry hearth and leather bellows. Independent package critics re-examined all nine replacements. Eight of the nine had retained checkpoint geometry through the first candidate pass. [Director findings and image evidence](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/art_direction.md)

The director signed all 101 listed families on the final 19 painted and 34 grayscale sheets. All 71 packages cleared publication. The complete history now contains 393 numbered package rounds and 2,650 individual model evaluations. Fresh reviews of previously accepted assets brought 27 more models to four or more lifetime evaluations. The Escalations table now lists all 211 such models with their histories and resolutions. No round-two model needed four evaluations within this repair. [Director’s signed sheet hashes](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/art_direction_approval.json)

Two complete builds on this M1 produced identical SHA-256 hashes for all 4,939 outputs. Both published 1,034 models, 2,130 runtime lookup keys and 314 contact sheets. The inventory digest is `7f2d87557eafb7598dd438b7ed58112cea642c0a22d1485f9c6a05007bacc7db`. All 24 AssetForge tests and 19 headless building integration tests passed. The 200-building test assembled 409,720 vertices and 173,105 triangles into four single-material meshes. The 1,034 models use one shared material and two shared textures. Their iPhone ASTC mip chains total a calculated 666,384 bytes. Unity Editor import, glTFast in a player, GPU draw calls, device memory and frame time, and cross-machine reproducibility remain unverified. The simulation suite was unchanged and its three previously recorded failures were not rerun in this repair. [Build inventory](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/rc4/rebuild_inventory.json.gz) · [Tests and verification limits](BUILDING_ART_VERIFICATION.md)

The independent final diff reviewer closed both findings. The audit now rejects incomplete inventories and stale post-checkpoint approvals. The grayscale sheet captions now show chronological age intervals, including early unlocks. The reviewer reproduced all 908 checkpoint views, 672 final grayscale views and 336 final captions pixel for pixel, and checked all 2,650 score rows, 211 escalation histories and 56 retained-pair entries. [Final independent diff review](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/rc4/diff_review.md)

The art director also observed a close silhouette in the **Elf FishingHut**, outside the 101 listed race/type families. Its Industrial and Future exports both place a leaf-roofed hut, skiff, frame and hanging pot on a round timber raft. Both directions share 1,141 of 2,691 corners (42.40%). Both GLBs match `7e905bb`. This inherited visual issue is separate from the retained over-60% comparisons above. [Art director’s observation](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/art_direction.md)

## Coordinator repair round 1 at checkpoint `7e905bb`


Checkpoint `91863de` contained 1,034 GLBs. The coordinator listed 61 era families with substantially repeated geometry, and identified Elf Herbalist separately in the visual comparison. The review also found 104 models above 65% for LOD1. Checking the stricter requested ceiling against the checkpoint identified 376 above 60%, including those 104. Houses ship **two variants for every race and age**.

All 61 listed families now pass the directed LOD0 test across **390 era pairs**. The largest overlap is **462 of 1,962 corners (23.55%)**, from Human Industrial Harbor to Future Harbor. The separately named Elf Herbalist shares zero corners across its 12 directed comparisons. The audit rounds each indexed triangle corner to three decimals and checks both directions. The independent judges also compared each family at 64 pixels with atlas colour and emission disabled. [Measurements for each family and changed GLB](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/coordinator_round1.json) · [Unpainted comparison pages](../tools/AssetForge/previews/_sheets/era_geometry/)

| Race | Coordinator-listed families | Separately pictured | Additional families held by critics | Changed GLBs | Changed LOD0 | LOD1-only |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Human | 4 | 0 | 7 | 75 | 15 | 60 |
| Elf | 5 | 1 | 30 | 111 | 70 | 41 |
| Dwarf | 11 | 0 | 8 | 89 | 33 | 56 |
| Orc | 14 | 0 | 9 | 85 | 41 | 44 |
| Saurian | 27 | 0 | 3 | 100 | 58 | 42 |
| Shared wonders | 0 | 0 | 0 | 1 | 0 | 1 |
| **Total** | **61** | **1** | **57** | **461** | **217** | **244** |

The additional 57 families entered visual revision when critics found that an affected model still read as an earlier era. The lead retained accepted siblings. All 346 supplemental comparisons involving a redesigned model passed 60%. Saurian Industrial Hospital against Future Hospital was the largest at 18.31%. Of the 217 changed LOD0 meshes, 121 belong to the coordinator's families or the Elf Herbalist and 96 to named supplemental findings. At the end of round 1, the highest individual directed overlap was 3,468 of 3,492 corners (99.31%) between Human Industrial Granary and Future Granary. Both files matched the preceding checkpoint. Round 2 includes this family. The coordinator’s original script, which averages consecutive comparisons per family, reports no families above 80%. [Binary audit](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/coordinator_round1.json)

**No model exceeds the 60% LOD1 ceiling**. The highest ratio is exactly 60%, including Orc Ancient House at 510/306/144 triangles. The validator checks the serialized triangle counts with integer arithmetic. The shared reducer preserves prominent wall, roof and step planes and prevents closed shells from turning inward. Painted front, side and rear comparisons exposed damaged panels, steps and doorways and prompted individual authored LOD1 fixes. [Regression tests](../tools/AssetForge/tests/test_shared_pipeline.py) · [Surface and shell survey](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/surface_checks.json)

The first repair pass held 109 of 461 models. Revisions included a full-span Human harbor gantry, exposed Elf production machinery, a book roof and roller press in the Saurian archives, working Dwarf apothecaries, and new Orc civic and sacred plans. The repaired catalog has 153 additional package rounds and 773 new model evaluations. With checkpoint history preserved, that makes 333 package rounds and 2,479 evaluations. The lowest current scores are 91 for silhouette, 90 for race/age, 92 for construction, 90 for charm, 91 for colour and 99 for technical health. Individual records below identify the model, round, scores and original defects.

Three models reached a fourth evaluation during the coordinator repair. The Classical and Future Elf Markets needed a new market plan and visible merchandise. The Future Elf Well needed a new waterworks silhouette and a readable contrasting rim and water surface at LOD1. All three passed. Across the entire production history, 184 models received at least four individual evaluations. Three of those amassed that many evaluations through fresh checks without a recorded failed score. The historical Saurian Ancient Fishing Hut failed package rounds 1, 2 and 5 and passed in round 6. Its revisions seated the punt, lowered the water below the hull interior and separated the roof materials at phone size. Full histories and resolutions are listed under Escalations below. [Saurian food ledger](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/saurian_food.json) · [Elf commerce ledger](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/elf_commerce.json) · [Elf infrastructure ledger](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/elf_infrastructure.json)

The art director inspected 19 painted pages covering all 2,096 age previews and 17 grayscale pages covering 361 era models in 119 lineages from two angles at 64 pixels. A first-pass hold identified an ordinary Saurian Future Farm that resembled the Hydroponics Tower. The replacement spreads four planted beds and irrigation channels across the field, with a small solar sluice at one corner. The independent package critic scored it 95/95/93/93/91/99 in round 10. It shared at most 2.81% of its corners with another Farm era. [Art direction review](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/art_direction.md) · [Farm review history](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/saurian_food.json)

Two complete builds published 1,034 models, 2,130 runtime lookup keys and 297 contact sheets apiece. All 4,922 file hashes matched, with canonical inventory digest `a75e4f69514711b03cd11a718241748de3a697b4ff53dc560a02e8ce4ac31bf9`. All 19 AssetForge tests and all 19 headless integration tests passed. The 200-building headless case combined 420,470 vertices and 176,994 triangles into four single-material meshes. [Verification record](BUILDING_ART_VERIFICATION.md) · [Complete file hashes](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/coordinator_rebuild.json.gz)

The shared 1,024-square atlas and 256-square mask consume a calculated 666,384 bytes with the configured iPhone ASTC 6×6 mip chains. Unity Editor, actual glTFast import, GPU shader compilation, player draw calls, device memory and iPhone visual/performance results remain unverified. Cross-machine reproduction has not been tested. The earlier simulation suite had three failures in unmodified simulation code. [Verification record](BUILDING_ART_VERIFICATION.md)

The final whole-diff reviewer checked all 3,102 LODs, all 2,479 score rows, the current model and sheet approvals, and each hash in the two rebuild inventories. Both review findings are closed. The coordinator committed the round-one repair as `7e905bb` on `art/buildings`. [Independent diff review](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/coordinator_diff.md)

| [dwarf_wonders](../tools/AssetForge/reviews/dwarf_wonders.json) | 1 | `bld_dwarf_deep_forge_ancient_v0` | 94 | 92 | 85 | 90 | 92 | 96 | The four brass crossbars on the two front buttresses hover in LOD0. At their centre z positions of -0.954 and -0.714, the stone incline is about 0.198 and 0.446 tiles high, while the bar centres are 0.290 and 0.550. Seat the underside of each bar in the slope across its width and inspect a low oblique view for daylight. / At LOD0 and LOD1 the three stock ingots straddle the right-hand buttress edge at x=0.775. They read as short horizontal fittings sticking out of its side. Move the entire pile onto clear, level ground beside the buttress, with the top ingot resting across the two lower ingots. Retain a clear operator route. / The open, dark flues in LOD0 and LOD1 turn into two solid orange disks in LOD2. The low mesh fills the top rim at full height with the forge patch. Preserve a recessed dark inner face or dark cap inside the low-poly rim, then compare all three meshes from above so the pair of stacks remains visually continuous. |
